1906.] HANKOW. 63 (Received 2 ist January 1907.) CUSTOM HOUSE, HANKOW. 1 6th January 1907. SIR, I.&mdash;In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circulars Nos. 21 of 1869, 14 of 1872, and 6 of 1875, I now transmit to the Statistical Department, at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade of this port for the fourth quarter of the year 1906. 96 CUSTOMS GAZETTE. [OCT.-DEC., (Received 18th January 1907.) CUSTOM HOUSE, NANKING, 15th January 1907. * 1906.] kiaochow. 279 (Received 31st January 1907J CUSTOM HOUSE, KIAOCHOW, 28th January 1907. sir, I.&mdash;a. Jn conformity with the instructions contained in your Circulars Nos. 21 of 1869, 14 of 1872, and 6 of 1875, I now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade of this port for the fourth quarter of the year 1906. b. The Provisional Customs Arrangement between China and Germany, under which the Kiaochow Custom House was opened at Tsingtau on the 1st July 1899, was amended by the additional Convention of the 1st December 1905. Under the latter Agreement the Free area, which until then comprised the whole Pachtgebiet, was on tlie ist January 1906 restricted to the Harbour territory. Goods arriving are stored and re-exported from there free of Duty. On leaving the Free port area Imports and Exports pay the same Duties as at the Treaty Ports. II.
